5a1(1): @#/ O /'khor ba la 'di lta ste|gsang ba chen mo byams pa'i snying po zhes bya ba'i sangs rgyas dang /gsang ba chen po'i snying po zhes bya ba'i sang rgyas dang /gsang ba'i 
5a2(1): ba chen po dga' ba'i snying po zhes bya ba'i sadas rgyas dang /gsang ba che po snyoms par kyi snying po zhes bya ba'i sangasargyas dang /bsang ba chen po bde ba'i dbang phyug ma zhes bya ba'i rnal 
5a3(1): 'byor ma chen mo dang /gsang ba chen mo 'joms byed dbang phyug ma zhes bya pa'i rnal 'byor ma chen mo dang /gsung ba chen mo dga'i ba'i dbang phyug ma chen mo dang /gsang pa chen mo khyab 
5a4(1): pa'i dbang phyug ma zhes bya  ba'i dbang phyug chen mo lastsogs ste/shin tu chad myed pa'i 'khor dang /gzhan yang rol pa chen po'i 'khor 'di lta ste/rol pa chen po sangs rgya ya k+Sha he ru

5a6(1): dang /rol pa chen po'i rnal 'byor ma rak+Sha shwa rI ga zhes bya ba dang /rol ( )pa chen po'i rnal 'byor mab+hig k+Sha rI gA zhes bya ba dang /rol pachen mo'i rnal 'byor ma g+hi sha shA ri ka zhes bya ba

5b1(1): byed chen mo'i 'byor ma pe kA ri zhes bya ba dang /'joms byed chen po'i rnal ma b+hi shwa ri ka zhes bya ba lastsogs te/shin tu 'jigs byed chen po'i 'khor dang /gzhan yang 'joms byed chen po'i 'khor
5b2(1): 'di lta ste|'joms byed chen po'i sangs rgyas trag kA he ru ka zhes bya ba dang /'jigs byed chen po'i 'khod sangs rgyas hri sha he ru ka zhes bya ba dang/'joms byed chen po'isangs rgyas ka kA he
5b3(1): ru zhes bya ba dang /'jom byed chen po'i sangs rgyas b+ha kA he ru ka zhes bya dang /'joms byed che po'i rnal 'byor ma trag sha shwa ri ka zhes bya ba dang /'jomam byed chen po'i rnal 'byor ma
5b4(1): hri sha shwa ri ka zhes bya ba ngang /'joms byed chen pa'i rnal 'byor ma ka sha shwa ri ka zhes bya bdang /'joms byed chenapo'i rnal 'byor ma b+ha kA ri ka lastsogs te/shin tu
5b5(1): 'joms byed chen mo'i 'khor dang /gzhan yag za byed chen po'i 'khor 'di lta ste/za byed chen po'i sangs rgyasaru ti he ru ka zhes bya ba dadang /za byed chen mo'i sangs rgyas rag
5b6(1): ti he ru ka zhes bya ba da /za byed chen po'i sangasargyas kha hi he ru ka zhes bya ba dan/za byed chen po'i sangs rgas sa he ru ka zhes bya ba da/za byed chen po'i rnam 'byor ma ru ti kA ri ka zhes
5b7(1): bya ba dang /za byed chen po'i rnal 'byoram rag ti shwa ri ka zhes bya ba dad/za byed chen po'i rnal byor ma kha hi shwa ri zhes bya ba dang /za byed chenamo'i rnal 'byor ma sa ha shwa ri ka zhes bya ba
5b8(1): lastsogs te/rnal 'byor gyi 'khor rab tu mang zhing bsam gyis myi khyab pa'i he ka mtha' yas pa sha stag rnams dang /gzhan yang gsang ba'i bu mo'i 'khor 'di lta ste/gsang ba'i bu

8a7(1): rnam grol de nyid nges pa'i cho 'phrul ston//myi shes blun rmongs 'dam 'byings 'gro ba rnams//sdug bsngal rgya mtshos bcings pa thabs kyis grol//ma rig mun pa ldongs pa don gyi phyed 
8a8(1): 'khor ba'i rgya mtshor rol pa'i gru yi sgrol//de bzhin gshegs pa kun gyi thugs chen po//ye shes rgyal ba don gyi snying po mchog dang ldan//rnal 'byor chen po rdo rje kun 'dus rgyud/'dus pa sna tshogs 
8b1(1): la rgya cher bshad du gsol//e ma ho;zhes nges par btud te gsol ba btab po//de nas bcom ldan 'das bsad ba mchog gi bdag po des rdo rje rol pa'i de kho na nyid kyi glu des gsol pa btab mkhyen

10a1(1): @#//rdo rje rgyal po'i tshogs bdag dgongs su gsol//chos kyi rgyal po myi g.yo rgyal tshul mdzad//rgyal mchog rgyal pos chos kyi rgyal po'i sku//'dus

13b5(1): myi 'gyur|de 'drar sems kyang de ltar blta//sems nyid gzhan du ma grub ste//de bzhin chos kyang grub ma yin//sems dang chos nyid gnyis myed de//sems nyid gang yin chos nyid de//
13b6(1): chos nyid kyang de bzhin no//chos sems gnyis myed ldan gyurd pa//bla myed byang chen por bshad//sems ni namakha' lta bu ste//rnam par bral gang myi rtog/de bzhin sa gzhi chen po ltar//
13b7(1): bla myed byang chub myu gu bskyed//mye'i rang bzhin ltar snang bas//rmongs pa'i bud shing bsregs par byed//rlung gi rang bzhin yin pa na//myi shes pa yi rdul kun sel//chu'i rang bzhin yin

14a1(1): rang bzhin pas/sdug bsngal rgya mtsho rnams par gcod//nyi ma lta bur snang ba'i mun khang snang stong sel bar byed/ri rab lta bu'i rang bzhin

14a3(1): 'das//gnyis su snang ba ma yin te//sems kyi rang bzhin bzang ngan las//gnyis kyi 'du shes phye ba stan//sems las yang dag ye shes 'byung //de bzhin bde

14a7(1): cad du//skyes pa bud myed ma ning 'byung //'di las 'od dang 'od zer 'byung //nyon mongs gdung 'dod chags pa 'byung //ri dang skye bo de bzhin 'byung //nam mkha' dang ni rlung dag dang //
14a8(1): bsreg pa'i mye ni rab tu 'byung //'di la sa dang chu dang ni//rdo dang sa bon myu gu 'byung //de bzhin klog dang skar mda' dang //nyi ma'i sbyin rnams 'byung //de bzhin spyod dang myi spyod kun 
14b1(1): thams cad thams cad 'byung bar 'gyur//dper na namakha'i rang bzhin las//sprin dang na bun khu blangs dang //zla ba ba'i nyin skar tshogs dag/snang yang nam mkha' bzhin du gnas|
14b2(1): de bzhin sems kyi mkha' las//mtshan ma'i chos 'phrul ci byung yang //chos nyid nam mkha' bzhin gnas par//rnal 'byor can gyis blta bar bya//de lta bu'i sems nyid ni//rtogs
14b3(1): dang ma rtogs rang bzhin las//lta ba'i khyad par so sor snang //rtog pa can gyi ngo gang la//rtog pa 'phrul pa'i ngo bor snang //kun tu nyer 'jog lam 'tshol la//kun tu rdul
14b4(1): gyi phung por snang //tshogs spyod snying rje nyer 'jog la//thams cad sgyu ma'i rang bzhin ltar//de ltar nyid mnyam par shes gnas na//mnam kha'i rang bzhin mthong bar gnas//ye shes

14b7(1): 'di bo zhes ni bsrungs du myed//bsrung ba'i rnam grangs ci snyed cig/dus gsum rgyal bas gsungs mod kyang //bsrung ba'i mdo ni cig tu 'dus//namakha' lta bu'i rnal

15a1(1): @#/ O /dor byed|srung dang bral ba'i chos nyid ni/bsrung du myed pas de bzhin bzhag/de ni bzhin pa'i dam tshig la//ji bzhin pa'i blo gzhag ste|brjod

15a7(1): dgos//gang zhig 'dod te ci zhig sgrub//ci spyad ci bsgrubs ci brtsal yang //sdug bsngal nyid de byang chub myed//dmyal ba sangs rgyas chen po la//sdug bsngal byang chub li nyid de//nyan 
15a8(1): thos rang rgyal sa gnyis dang //theg pa'i khyad par ci bstan pa//byed rtsol bcas pa thams cad 'khrul//bla myed byang chub dges su cha//gang zhig brtsal myed bde chen don//sems kyi klong 'di ma

15b3(1): bral//brtsal myed ye nas lhun gyis grub//mtha' dbus myed pa'i bdal pa che//phyi nang myed ye gsal khyab//rkyon yon myed par lhun gyis rdzogs//bsgrub du myed pa'i

15b6(1): spyod//myi dmyigs pa la nam mkha' spyod//de ltar spyod pa kun spyod kyang //dam pa'i don de spyod mchog nyid//dam par gyurd pa thams cad la//dam pa'i spyod pa de las myed//skyes 
15b7(1): bu mchog gi de skad bshad//the tsom myed par shes par gyis//ma bcos byang chub de bzhin nyid//sgom du myed pa'i chos nyid la//sgom pa dmyigs su myed pa ste//ting 'dzin zhes ni 
15b8(1): bdags su myed//sgoms kyang bskyed pa ma yin la//de bzhin ma sgoms stor bral myed//bsgom chos sna tshogs cir snang yang //sgoms nas de'i rang myi gnas//de bas byis pa'i spyod yul yin//dper na byis pa byis pa'i rang gyis//
17a1(1): @#/ O /grib ma yis ;shin tu bsgribs par gyurd pa na//sprin chen 'dus pa'i phung po la//kha'i dbugs kyi byas nas ;sprin gsal nyi 'od gsal 'dod 'dra//de phyir rnal 'byor dbang phyug gis ;sems nyid

111a2(1): yang bdud rtsi chen po ni//gong ma bzhin du legs sbyar nas//man 'dal tshad dang mthun pa la//pad ma dmar po 'od kyi mtha'//phyi rim zla gam dmar po bzhi//rdo rje 'bar bas mdzes par bskor//
111a3(1): mye lce 'bar ba'i 'phreng ba ni//zla gam mthar zad bri bar bya//rin cen ge sa 'phreng ba na//pad+ma'i lce la bskor bar bya//sgo bzhi rin cen 'bar bar byas//phyag rgya 'bar ba'i lha bkod la//
111a4(1): dbus su gser phor sul med la//dung chen 'khor ba can gyis sbyar//shar du shel por sul bsgred la//rin cen mdzod spun gsal bar sbyar//lho ru rin cen sul mangs la//
111a5(1): rin cen lo 'dab rgyas par sbyar//nub tu rgya phor zla gam la//bzhin mtshams rin cen 'bur gnyis sbyar//byang du gse phor rgya gram la//gtsug tor rin cen 'bur bzhi rgyas//rtsa ba 
111a6(1): lnga ni phyogs sbyar//sgo lnga'i gtso bo rigs su sbyar//dbus nas shar lho nub byang du//ka ko la dang du ru sku//'dza ti pha la kha dri zhim//lce po'i rim par sbyar//de bzhin gong gi rim 
111a7(1): pa bzhin//a ru ka dang ga pur dang //gur gum pa dam lig tur//de nas ra sa yan rnams//thams cad rigs dang //las dang 'byung ba sbyar bar bya//sa bon lnga ni rim par sbyar//dpal 
111a8(1): dang ldan pa'i snod nang du//yab yum snying po rim bzhin sbyar//dbus dang pad+ma'i lce la dgod//nas la stsogs mdzes par gzhag/dur khrod dzas dang kra shis rdzas//'byord pa'i yo byad 
111b1(1): thams cad bshams //de nas byin phab dngos grub gsol//thams cad rnams par sbyar bar bya//dam pa de nyid gsang chen ni//rigs brgya rigs gcig gi/thams cad sbyang ba'i mchog tu
111b2(1): bya|de nas khrol bu sbyar ba na//brtsal sngo bsdu dang dbye ba dang //khrus bya ba dang bshags pa dang //yon tan brjod dang byin dbabs dang //de nas skad cig thad cig gam//zhag cig dag gis sbyang
111b3(1): ba bya|rtags dang byung na der 'byongs pa'o//phye ma'i mchog tu sbyar ba 'di//snod kyi sman rnams shes rab mas//bsdus te btags pa'i cho ga yang //yum gyis bzung te yab kyi btags//rigs 
111b4(1): 'dzind rtsal can dbab cing drang //so so'i las kyi sngags brjod cing //gtan gtun thabs dang shes rab dang //sman nyid byang chub sems su sbyar//rdo rje klu nyid lha mos blang //kun tu bzang mo'i 
111b5(1): gzhal yas su//thabs dang shes rab ldan pa las//'khor ba'i bdud rtsa sman du sbyar//ye shes lnga ru gsal ba las//'bus bu sku gsung thugasu rdzogs//'chi myed bdud rtsa khams 
111b6(1): gsum 'khor ba'i//gcod snying sbyangs mtshan ma btags phyir//gtsal nas ni/rdo rje dpal ldan kha sbyor blugs//kha dog rigs lnga'i ras kyis dkri//rdo rje sngags kyi gzungs kyis gdags//
111b7(1): dpal chen sku lngar mnga' gsol te//ye shes lnga'i dbang bskur bya//ma 'dal dag tu dgod pa bya//kun gyis bskor zhing dngos grub dbang //rtsa dang sman gyi bdag po dang //rtags ldan rigs
111b8(1): ni phra mo dag/bsgrub pa'i 'bru mchog pa dag/bkru shis lnga'i gos kyis rgyas par klubs//gzungs thag gnyer can rnams la rtad//ting 'dzin mye yis rnam par sbyang //rtags chen lnga ni gang 
112a1(1): @#/ O /byung yang |sgyur te dngos grub blang bar bya'o//bdud rtsi sgra la drangs na shi//dpal ldan pad ma ldan pa ru//thams cad bdud rtsi ras yan//kha klong yangs ba'i 
112a2(1): bde chen rdzogs//rang nyid bdud rtsi'i dkyil 'khor rdzogs//gzhan nas bsgrub pa ci yang myed//'jug la sogs thams cad kyang //tshul bzhin du ni rgyas par bya//ces gsungs so//bdud rtsi 
112a3(1): phye ma'i bsgrub pa rnams par bshad pa'o//de nas yang sems dpa' rdo rje des//'di skad gsungaso//'dus pa chen po'i rnal 'byord pa rnams//thams cad de 'drang sbyar nas kyang //man 'dal
112a4(1): 'khor lo rtsibs brgyad pa//phyag rgya'i lha rnams rdzogs byas nas//sman sbyang btags brtsags gzhal byas nas//sbyang cang 'o ma gong bur sbyar//bse phor mtshan ldan nang du gzhag/legs
112a5(1): par kha sbyar ras kyis dkro//gong ma bzhin du kun sbyar nas//dngos grub mchog rnams bdag gir bya//ces gsungs so//bdud rtsi ri lu cho ga rnam par bshad pa'o////de nas yang
112a6(1): sems dpa' rdo rje des 'di skad ces gsungs//'dus pa chen po'i rnam 'byord pa//skyo ma'i cho ga 'di lta ste//thams cad gong ma bzhin sbyar nas//mtshan ma gsal po byung ba dang //phye ma chu

112a8(1): gtsang;sbrang chang ram chang //rgun cang 'o ma 'ogs pa//bcud kyi khu ba thams cad sbyar//rin cen bu ga mtshan ldan du//blugs te kha sbyar rgyas btab nas//'phro 'du bzlas brjod gong ma bzhin;;

112b4(1): rnams 'jom par nus//drug dang bdun dang brgyad rnams la//bdud rtsi bcud kyis 'tsho bar 'gyur//rgu dang bcu las zag pa zad//bcu gcig rigs 'dzin nam mkha' 'phur//zhes gsungs so////

112b6(1): cho ga chen po ni//'di ltar khong du chud par bya//sbrang rtsi bu ram gu 'bru rnams//lo'i 'tsho bar sbyar nas kyang //kha dog mtshan ldan dmar po'i ba//lo'i 'tsho ba bzung zhing 'brel//sman gyi rgyal mo ram 
112b7(1): nye bas //rgyun tu g.yo ba'i 'o ma de//rigs 'dzin zas kyi mchog tu 'gyur//bdud rtsi'i rgyal mo ram nye ba//phye ma'i mchog tu byas nas kyang //lo'i 'tsho ba sgyur byas te//sus myi mthong gi sa phyogs
112b8(1): su //bsgrub rdzas de dag bkram nas kyang //bdud rtsi bsgrub pa de bzhin bya//dus la bcas pa'i chos rnams la//sgrub rdzas de dag thams cad kun//ba'i mas 'de gur bya//cha re bskyed cing 
113a1(1): @#/ O /bza' bar bya//zos pa'i yon tan bsam myi khyab zag pa zad nas nam mkha' 'gro//ces gsungs so//bdud rtsi 'de gu sgrub pa rnams par bshad pa'o////de 
113a2(1): nas yang sems dpa' rdo rje des 'di skad ces gsungs so//'dus pa chen po rnal 'byord pa//'di yang gong ma ltar byas te//de dang myi mthun grub rdzas rnams//bdud rtsir gyurd pa'i sman 
113a3(1): rnams kun ( )thams cad thams cad bsdu bar bya//til chen la logs zhun chen mtho//thams cad bsdus shing sbyar byas nas//gnas kyang gong ma bzhin brtsal te//bdud rtsi bkram nas bsgrub

113a5(1): rdzogs pa dang //dngos grub mchog ni blang bar bya//gzhan pa gzhan gyi sde pa la//lo'i bshugs te phrad myi bya//'tsho ba bdud rtsa sman gyis sbyar//bdud rtsi grub pa'i rtags chen ni//ba spu la/( )nga( )
113a6(1): dang gzi mangs 'bar//kham gyi zas bral bde bar 'tsho//shes rab gsal zhing thogs pa myed//zag pa zad nas nam mkhar 'gro//zhes gsungs so//bdud rtsi zhun chen gyi bsgrub pa bshad pa'o//
113a7(1): de nas yang bdud rtsi rdo rje des 'di skad ces gsungs so//'dus pa chen po'i rnal 'byord pa//blon po'i rdzas la sman bsgrubs na//thams cad gong ma bzhin sbyar nas//man 'dal thig le lnga

113b2(1): kyi rgyal po rdo rje bcod pa kun 'dus rig pa'i mdo las//bdud rtsi bsgrub pa'i le'u ste/nyi shu bdun pa'o////de nas yang sems dpa' rdo rje dam pa des 'di skad ces gsungaso//bsgrub
113b3(1): pa'i thabs snying pa'i gsang chen thugs kyi bsgrub pa zhes bya ba de bshad do//'dus pa chen po'i rnal 'byord pa//chos thams cad dbyings su shes par bya ste//'khor ba'i chos thams cad ni dbyings
113b4(1): su 'dus la//mya ngan las pa'i chos thams cad ye shes su 'dus pa'o//de gnyis su myed pa'i chos nyid byang chub kyi sems dpa' chen por 'dus par gyis shig/de nas na chos thams cad ni
113b5(1): thums ye shes kyi rang bzhin du 'dus par bzhin du 'dus par shes par gyis shig/gnyis su myed de//de ltar kun rdzob 'di ltar rig par bya ste//gnyis su myed pa'i don la 'jug par bya'o//'dus pa
113b6(1): chen po'i rnal 'byord pa/dam pa'i don kyi chos rnams kun//thams cad rang gi sems la rdzogs//kun rdzob chos rnams gang yin pa//lus la rdzogs dag la rdzogs par bshad//de nas chos 'di thams
113b7(1): cad kyang /rtsa ba rang la rdzogs pa ste//de dag bsgrub pa'i thabs mchog kyang //rnam par rtog pa bdul bas la nas//sgrub pa'i rgyal po yab yum gnyis//don la mkhas par byas nas ni//so so'i thugs la
113b8(1): thig le dag/zla gam dkar mar tab par bya//de'i rdzas mchog chen po yang //thabs kyi ga pur chen po dang //shes rab gsang ba'i tsan dan gnyis//dkar mar zla gam chen por bya//yab kyi thugs gar dkar
114a1(1): @#/ O /po la;thig le kha ni mar la blta ba'o//yab kyi thig le'i dbus su ni//de gsal par yi dbus su bri//dbus su o gsal nang du hU~M//yum gyi thig le'i dbus su ni/pad ma'i dbus su zla 
114a2(1): ba bri;dbus su o gsal nang duhU~M//de ltar gsal par gyurd pa dang //kun tu bzang po yab dang yum//gnyis su myed par sgyur par bya//ting 'dzin gsal par gyurd pa dang//rang las byung ba'i bdud rtsi yis//
114a3(1): gsang ba gnyis kyi mchod par bya//rang las byung ba'i dngos grub dag/rang nyid la ni bstim bar bya//de ltar bsgrub pa rtsor byed nas//bdud rtsi yo byad thams cad dang //bcud kyi mchog tu gyurd pa rnams//lo'i
114a4(1): skad cig par du ni//bdud rtsi'i yo byad rnams la brten//de nas lo'i skad cig ni//gcig gam gnyis kyi par dag ni//rang las byung ba'i rdzas rnams las//'phags pa mchod cing rang gis bza'//des ni drod

114a6(1): gyurd|rtags ni cho 'phrul 'byung ba'i//rin ces phung po'i 'dod pa dang //sa rdo la stsogs byas nas grub//chu dang mkha' la 'gro 'dod na//brtsams pa myi 'bing thogs myed 'gro//mye'i phung po 'jil 
114a7(1): 'dod na||bsdigs pa'i 'dzub mo phyar nas na//bden pa bsgo ba byas pas 'gru ba//de la sogs pa thams cad nub//rtags thob phan cad sangs rgyas yin//zhes gsungs so//gsang chen thugs 
114a8(1): kyi bsgrub pa rnams par bshad pa'o//rgyud kyi rgyal po chen po rdo rje bkod pa kun 'dus rig pa'i mdo las//bka' gsang 'khor bsgrub pa'i le'u ste/nyi shu brgyad pa'o////de nas bcom ldan 'das sem+sa
114b1(1): pa rdo rje dam pa des//rdo rje me long 'bar ba zhes bya ba'i ting 'dzin la snyoms par bzhugs so//de nas 'dus pa'i tshogs kyis chos thams cad chos thams cad me'i snang bar mthong ngo //de nas 'khor 
114b2(1): rnams kyis bstod pa rab tu bstod pa//a ma'o sems pa che ba ste//ting 'dzin chen po snang ba che//cho 'phrul chen po snang tshogs che//thabs mchog khyo la phyag 'tshal lo//chos kun me klong nyid du snang 
114b3(1): me nyid ye shes gsal ba'i gling //'bar 'pho gsal dngos po thams cad bsregs//bde chen dbyings su kun rdzogs pa//dpa' bo'i khyod kyis mdzad lags//thugs rje chen po a la la//'jigs 
114b4(1): byed chen po a la la//dgyes pa chen po a la la//dgra po chen po a la la//za byed chen po a la la//pa' po chen po'i byin rlabs kyis//bdag cag rnams la bshad du gsol//mye'i rang bzhin 
114b5(1): chen po dag/bdag cag rnams la bsha du gsol//zhes gsol ba btab ba las//sems pa rdo rje dgyes te 'di skad ces gsungs so//'dus pa chen po'i rnal 'byord pa//rdo rje mye'i rang bzhin 
114b6(1): dag/bshad kyis khyod rnams gus par nyon//rdo rje mye'i las mchog 'dis//rnal 'byor thabs su bde ba sbyar bas//rgyal ba kun mnyes kun bsregs skongs//don grub las kyis mchog tu bshad//
114b7(1): skye myed rnam dag hU~M khung du//chos can skye ba'i yam shing la//spros myed byang chub ye shes myes//mtshan ma'i bag chags bsregs sbyangs thim//rnam dag ba ga'i ha~M khung du//chags pa'i yam shing sbyangs 
114b8(1): pa la//bde chen yab yum me sbyar sbyar ste//'khor ba'i dngos po bsregs sbyangs thil//dpal chen zhal gyi ha~M khung du//nyon mongs lnga'i yam shing la//ye shes lnga'i mye sbar ste//gzung 'dzin 
115a1(1): @#/ O /rdzas rnams bsregs sbyangs thim//snang srid nam mkha'i ha~M khung du //'byung ba bzhi'i yam shing la//phyag rgya sna tshogs mye sbar te//snang srid dngos po sregs sbyangs thim/࿒
115a2(1): las bzhi so so'i khyad par gyis//phyogs dang kha dog dbyings dang rdzas//ci ltar yin pa de ltar sbyangs shar lho nub byang dkar ser dmar thing ka nag po thal mdog sbyar//zlum po pad+ma le ce bzhi///
115a3(1): 'khor lo grub bzhi zur brgyad rgyas//gru gsum pa ga mkha' mngon spyod//gsum rtsegs mdangs dang ri mor mdzes//las kyi yam shing rdzas kyis rgyan//ye shes rdo rje'i las rgyud ldan//bla ma

115a7(1): dngos por bzhir snang ste//chos can skyen dang 'byung gnas dang //rang bzhin nyid las cho 'phrul ni//skye ba nyid du 'dod pa yin//'di ni skye ba'i chos can te//rang byung sreg rdzas rang bzhin thim//nyon mongs lnga'i

115b1(1): brgyad gyi bsreg rdzas rnams//ye shes gnas kyi ma brgyad do//yul gyi g.yeng pa rnam pa brgyad//dmyigs myed dbyings kyi bye brag bsnyad//snye bzhi las kyi bsreg rdzas rnams//thams cad bzhi'i glong du 
115b2(1): thim;thabs mchog la sogs rgya mtsho'i gnas//so so'i yon tan thams cad dang //rdo rje glu yis brjod nas kyang //de dag mnyes pa'i mchod pa brtsam//mchod pa thams cad rdzogs pa dang //sgrib byad

115b5(1): blang ;ro'i rdzas rnams bsregs sbyangs na//ro sngags kyis btab nas kyang //shin tu dri zhim sna tshogs kyis//ro la byug cing bkru bar bya//bdud rtsa chen po'i khu ba yis//ras las byugs pas ro dkris 
115b6(1): te;;ro'i gnas rnams gang yin pa//sngags kyi sbyor ba kun bkod nas//phyag rgya'i mtshan mas gdab bar bya//rgyal ba'i dkyil 'khor gang dag gi/gzhung yin phyag rgya rnams//gang du byung ba ma 
115b7(1): pa'o//sdong bu grungs kyang de bzhin bya//sdong bu de la lha rnams kyi//ma nor snga ba'i snying po dag/ras chen dag la bri te gtags//ro'i sbyor ba rdzogs pa dang //ro'i phyir bdun thams cad la//'khor ba'i 
115b8(1): bag chags kun bkod de//ras sam shing bu shog shog la//'khor ba'i sa bon thams cad dkod//nyon mongs lnga dang dus gsum bcas//skye ba'i lam lnga rgyud drug rnams//mying ngam gzugs 
116a1(1): @#/ O /su byas nas ni|gnas gnas dag tu dkod par bya//khams dang skye mched phung po lnga//gzung 'dzin mying du bcas pa rnams//brda'i tshul du bris nas ni//ro rnams khyab par dgod 
116a2(1): par bya//gnas ro'i phung po de//brten nas glegs ma bstegs du'i//steng du mkhas pas bzhag par bya//thab kyi cho ga brtsam pa ni//man dal 'khor yug tsam zhig la//'go dang kha khyer lugs bzhin
116a3(1): bya||dkyil 'khor gang dang gang dang yin pa'i//phyag rgya bzhugs pa'i pho brang dag/thig gis yul bcad so sor bya//de'i sting du shing rtsig pa//dbyibs ni gru bzhi gtams pa la//zur bzhi ka ba bzhi 
116a4(1): brtan bya||sum rtse g.yang thog par khar bcas//tha ma shing gis bcang bar bya//bar khang dag gi glegs ma la//ro'i bzhir phyogs shar du brten//ro'i steng gi khang pa la//ras sam shog 
116a5(1): shog la bris pa'i//phyag rgya'i dkyil 'khor gsal bar dgod//ro khang blo skor bzhi phye la//thugs rje gzung thag btags nas//phyogs bzhi'i bla ma bzhi la dbul//shing gi cho ga rdzog+sa
116a6(1): ba dang |shing gi phyed mtha' stegs bu la//phyag rgya'i lha rnams rim par dgod//stegs bu rdzogs par gyurd pa dang //rdo rje las kyis skos btab la//rdo rje rgyal po bla ma la//'khor ba 
116a7(1): bsreg pa mnga' gsol te//ting 'dzin ye shes rtse gcig pas//me'i 'du 'phro rgyun tu bya//rdo rje las kyi gar ma la//las rnams rdzogs pa'i mnga' gsol te//las kyi 'phreng ba rdzogs 
116a8(1): par bya//rdo rje chen po'i bla ma zhig/ro'i nges pa'i mtha' bzung nas//las kyi nyes pa bgrang zhing bshags//rdo rje bskabs phyir bla ma yis//bdud rtsi skabs kyi mchod pa bsgreng //rdo rje
116b1(1): zo gar klu tshigs kyis//skabs kyi glu gar mchod pa dga'//rol mos sna drangs mchod pa dag/rdo rje gnas kyi smon lam ni//bskabs dang las kyi mjug du gdab//rdo rje las kyi ltas 'dzin

150a6(1): rig lta ba'i dbyings//'dod rgu rang la yon tan 'char ba'i phyir//rgyu 'brus bu gzhan la rag ma lus pas za ba//dper na ma dros mtsho dkyil me 'bar ba//nam mkha' dbyings nas gsal 
150a7(1): ba'i chu yis ni//bskal bar de la gnod mi nus pa ltar//rang sems chos nyid rang byung ye shes la//'khor bar 'khyams kyang sgrib pas gos pa myed//rin cen me 'od nam mkhar thon 
150a8(1): pa'i tshe//rgya mtsho nyid kyang bdud rtsir 'gyur bzhin//rang gi byang chub sems nyid rtogs tsam na//dug lnga nyid kyang ye shes chen por 'char//rdzogs pa chen por ye shes rting rdzogs

150b2(1): gdags phyir//ston pa kun rig sems kyi rgyal po la//bden pa'i tshig gis 'di skad ces gsol to//dug lnga ma spangs rang grol ci ltar byung //che ba'i yon tan myi 'byung gang gis

150b4(1): thugs kyis bstan du gsol//de skad gsol pa'i tshig gsan thugs kyis bka' stsal pa//dug lnga ma spangs rang grol ye shes ni//rin cen 'od kyis mun pa myed pa ltar//'khor ba 
150b5(1): thog mtha' myed gcad don gyi phyir//ngo bo nyid kyi sku ni rab rtogs pas//gdod nas ma rig dag pas gti mug bral//de 'ang 'dus pa'i rdo rje sems dpa' nyon//ma rig myed phyir zhe sdang 
150b6(1): yul myi dmyigs//de phyir rang sems snying po chos sku las//snga phyir zhen pa'i dbang gis zhe sdang na//kha dog yul la mnar myed pa'i phyir//ba mo la ni nyi ma shar ba bzhin//phyi 'brel 
150b7(1): gcad pa'i man ngan dran par bya//de yang dad can 'khor rnams yid kyis nyon//'dod chags skye ba'i yul ni sgo lnga 'byung ba'i sems//phyi rol zang zing nor rkam chags la//bung ba

177b2(1): thig le spros myed dam//thams cad kun kyang thig le'i rang bzhin na//ston pa'i ston pas ci zhig bstan par mdzad//'khor du 'khor pa'i don ni ci ltar 'khor//'khor la ston pas ci zhig
177b3(1): ston par byed//dus dang gnas ni ci lta bu zhes zhus so//de nas byang chub kyi sems kun byed rgyal po des//sems dpa' rdo rje la 'di skad ces gsungs so//kye sems dpa' chen po yid sgra 
177b4(1): la chug shig/ngas don sgrar bshad kyis//dkye sems dpa' chen po//sems nyid kun byed//rgyal po nga nyid ni//chos rnams kun gyi snying po ste//snying po ma spos ye nas thig le yin/
177b5(1): thig le ye nas spros pa myed pa'i don//ston pa dang ni bstan pa 'khor dus gnas//nga las byung bas ye nas thig le ste//nga'i rang bzhin ye nas thig le yin//zhes gsungs so//
177b6(1): byang chub kyi sems kun byedargyal po las gleng bzhi'i le'u ste dang pa'o////de nas byang chub kyi semasakun byed rgyal po'i zhal du 'khod pa//chos thams cad nyid las byung 
177b7(1): ba zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la gnas par mdzad do//de nas 'khor gyi rang bzhin las//sems dpa' rdo rje gnas//ston pa byang chub kyi sems kun byed rgyal po'i zhal du
177b8(1): 'khod pa//kye ston pa'i ston pa kun byed rgyal po lags//'khyed 'khor du bdag las 'khod pa de//bdag ni 'tshal ba'i don ni ci lta bu zhig mchis//bdag 'tshal don ni 
178a1(1): @#//bdag ni 'tshal ba'i don ni ci lta bu zhig mchis//bdag 'tshal don ni khyed kyis bstan du gsol//de nas byang chub kyi sems kun byed rgyal po des//nyid kyi rang 
178a2(1): bzhin dang //nyid kyi ngo bo dang //nyid kyi thugs rje las//chos thams cad bkod pa ni//nyid kyi rang byung gi ye shes chen po cig las//rang byung gi ye shes lnga byung ste//'di lta ste/zhe sdang rang 
178a3(1): byung gi ye shes chen po dang //'dod chags rang 'byung gi ye shes chen po dang//gti mug rang byung gi ye shes chen pa dang //nga rgyal rang 'byung gi ye shes chenapo dang //'phra dog rang byung gi 
178a4(1): ye shes chen po dang //rang 'byung ye shes lnga po des//rgyan gyi rgyu chen po lnga ni byung //'jig cing ston pa'i khams chen po gsum ni bkod//rgyan kyi lus lnga ni//gcig tu blangs pa 'di lta ste/
178a5(1): rgyan gyi rgyu'i lus sa zhes bya ba'i lus dang //rgyan gyi rgyu'i lus chu zhes bya ba'i lus dang //rgyan kyi rgyu'i lus me zhes bya ba'i lus dang /rgyan gyi rgyu'i lus rlung zhes bya ba'i lus dang /
178a6(1): nam mkha' zhes bya ba'i lus lnga//gcig la lus thams cad blangs so//ye shes lnga'is rigs lnga so sor phye ste//rang byung ye shes zhe sdang gi rigs dang //rang byung ye shes 'dod chags kyi rigs

178b2(1): kun byed rgyal po'i dgongs po ci zhig che//zhes zhus so//de nas byang chub kyi sems kun byed rgyal po'i des//slar gsungs pa//kye sems dpa' chen po nga'i dgongs pa ni//kun byed rgyal po'i byed mkhan

178b5(1): rang bzhin bstan pa ni//phun sum tshogs pa rnam pa gsum yin no//chos nyid chub pa'i rang bzhin bstan pa ni/dgos pa tshogs pa rnam pa gsum gyis ni nam mkha' bzhin du kun la khyab par gnas//

178b7(1): mngon du phyung ba'i le'u ste gnyis pa'o////de nas byang chub kyi sems kun byedargyal po des//chos thams cad kyi sngon rol tu byed pa por byung ba 'di gsungs so//kye sems dpa' chen po 
178b8(1): yid rna dbang la 'chug shig/rna dbang sgra la rtogs shig/kye nga ni kun byed rgyal po ste//nga ni kun byed byang chub sems//nga myed pa'i sngon rol du//chos rnams 'byung ba'i snying po myed//nga myed pa'i sngon
179a1(1): @#/ O /rol na//chos kun byed ba'i rgyal po myed//nga myed pa'i sngon rol na//ston pa bya ba ye nas myed//nga myed pa'i sngon ral na//bstan pa bya ba'i ye nas myed//
179a2(1): nga myed pa'i sngon rol na//'khor zhes bya ba'i ye nas myed//sems dpa' chen po khyod nyid kyang //nga'i ngo bo sprul pa yin//zhes gsungs so//de nas sems dpa' chen po des//byang chub

179a5(1): pa du ru yod//zhes gsungs so//de  nas byang chub kyi sems kun byed rgyal pos slar gsungs pa//kye rang 'byung ye shes kun kyi ston par cig pa'i rang bzhin rnam gsum snang ba las//ston pa 
179a6(1): grangs ni rnam pa gsum du byung //nga ni de bzhin nyid du gcig pa las//chos rnams kun kyi chos nyid de bzhin nyid//nga nyid byang chub sems las gzhan myed do//nga las byung ba'i ston pa sku gsum gyis//
179a7(1): 'khor la bstan pa rnam pa gsum tu bstan//nga'i ngo bo cig tu byung nas ni//nga'i rang bzhin gcig tu bstan pa'i phyir//kun byed nga'i 'khor du kun kyang gcig/nga las byung ba'i ston par sku gsuM 
179a8(1): gis//'khor la bye brag rnam pa gsum du yod//ces gsungs so//sems dpa' rdo rje zhus pa//ston pa'i ston pa kun byed rgyal po lags//khyod la byung ba'i ston pa sku gsumal//khyod 
179b1(1): sar 'gro ba'i lam ni yod dam myed//myed na khyod kyi sa ru phyin//kye sems dpa' chen po nyon cig/nga las byung ba'i ston pa sku gsum la//nga'i sa ru bgrod pa'i lam myed de//nga'i rang byung 
179b2(1): ye shes bstan pa yin//sku gsum ston pa de'i 'khor rnams la//rnam par thar pa'i lam yang rnam lnga ste//dus gsum sangs rgyas kun kyis bstan//rang 'byung ye shes lnga'i lam lnga ni//

179b4(1): ye shes rnam lngar bstan pa de//rang 'byung ye shes ni ci ltar 'byung ba lags//ye shes bya ba'i gtan tshigs ci la gdags//lam zhes bya ba'i don no gang la bgyi//'dod chags zhe sdang 
179b5(1): gti mug nga rgyal dang //'phra dog la sogs rnam pa lnga po de//lam lnga lags pa'i don ni bshad du gsol//zhes zhus so sems dpa' chen po//rang 'byung ye shes rnams pa lnga lam ni//rang 
179b6(1): 'byung rgyu rkyen myed par byung ba yin//ye shes 'gag pa myed par gsal ba yin//lam ni rgyu rkyen myed pas bsgrod myi dgos//myi dgod rang 'byung lnga la lam lngar bshad//'dod pa chos rnams bdag
179b7(1): du 'dod pa de la chags zhe ni skye ba myed pa'i rang bzhin la//sdang ba cho 'phrul sna tshogs de ldang //rti mug myi rtogs pa'i ting nge 'dzin kyis//chos nyid mnyam pa nyid la dbye bsal myed//nga ni ma nor 
179b8(1): ba'i byang chub sems//de bzhin nyid du ye nas rgyal byed//thams cad de bzhin nyid du gcig pa las//yin myin sgro bskur 'debs la 'phra dog byed//bgrod du myed pa'i lam lnga bstan pa yin//
180a1(1): @#/ O /kye sems dpa' chen po nyon cig/ci ltar snang ba'i chos rnams thams cad kun//rang 'byung ye shes rnam lnga 'di las byung //'dod pa'i khams kyang rang 
180a2(1): 'byung ye shes yin//gzugs kyi khams kyang rang 'byung ye shes yin//gzug sa myed khams kyang rang 'byung yin//kun byed rgyal po nga yis byas pa'i phyir//ngas byas thams cad ye/

180a4(1): ngas bshad//kun byed rgyal po'i rang bzhin las//chos nyid rnam pa gsum du bstan pa ste//ma skyes pa'i chos nyid dngos myi snang //de ni gzugs myed khams su bstan pa yin//skye ba'i

180b5(1): ba'i snying por bshad//nga ni snying po sems nyid kyis//chos rnams ma lus kun byed de//kun zhes bya ba chos rnams kun//chos kun gang dang gang zhe na//ston pa dang ni bstan pa

180b8(1): snying po byang chub sems de ni//rang 'byung ye nas rnam dag pas//kun byed rgyal pos byas pa kun//kun bzang nyid du rnam dag pas//byang zhes bya bar bshad pa yin//

181a3(1): rang 'byung ye shes des//snang srid snod bcud thams cad la//'jug cing gsal ba gsal bar gcod//des na sems zhes bar bshad//rgyu rkyen myed pa'i snying po des kun la dbang bsgyur

181a8(1): gcig las myed pa ste//bstan ni rnam pa gnyis su bstan//byung ni theg pa rgu ru byung //'dus pa rdzogs pa chen por 'dus//byung mi byang chub sems yin te//gnas ni chos nyid dbyings la 
181b1(1): gnas//gsal ni rig pa'i 'kha' la gsal//khyab ni snod bcud kun la khyab//bstan du mtshan ma'i dngos po myed//mngon du dmigs pa'i yul dang //shes su tshig tu brjod

181b3(1): ni sems nyid 'gag pa myed//nam mkha'i lta bu'i chos nyid la//nam mkha' lta bu'i dpe' yis mtshon//dmyigs su myed pa'i chos nyid la//dmyigs su myed par mtshon te bstan//
181b4(1): tshig tu brjod pa myed pa la//brjod du myed pa'i tshig brjod de//dmyigs myed don kyi ngo bor bstan//don kyi ngo bo 'brel pa ste//'di yis rang don brtags par gyis//'di yis

181b8(1): nga'i chos rnams mngon du phyung //nga'i ngo bo khyod la bstan//nga'i rang bzhin bstan nas ni//tshig dang sgras kyang don mtshon na//ngo bo bstan pas khyod kyis mthong //rang bzhin bstan pas a de rig/
182a1(1): tshig tu brjod nas nas don sgra thos//ces gsungs nas//cang myi smra bar chos thams cad kyi ngo bor bzhugs pas//sems dpa' rdo rje 'khor gi nang 
182a2(1): nas langs nas//byang chub kyi sems kun byed rgyal po'i zhal du mdangs phyung ste 'khod pas//kun byed rgyal pos//kye sems dpa' chen po//khyod mdangs kyis 'khod de//khyod kyi don

182a8(1): nga'i rang bzhin nga yis bkod pa yin//dus gsum sangs rgyas khams gsum sems can yang //nga'i rang bzhin khyod bstan pa yin//nga'i ngo bo ma skyes spros bral phyir//myi gnas 
182b1(1): spyod yul kun las 'das pa yin//myi snang ting 'dzin bsgom pa'i yul las 'das//nga'i rang bzhin khyod la myi snang byas//nga'i ngo bo khyod la myi snang byas pas//khams gsum

182b7(1): nga'i lung 'di bstan myi bya//nga'i lung 'di nges par bstan myi bya//dge sdig las la rgyu 'bras yod zer nas//yang dag la sgro dang skur btab pas//yang dag nga la yun du 'phrad myi 'gyur//
182b8(1): da ni bstan pa kun byed byang chub sems//byang chub sems ni kun byed rgyal po ste//dus gsum sangs rgyas byang chub sems kyis byas//khams gsum sems can byang chub sems kyis byas 
183a1(1): snang srid bcud byang chub sems kyis byas//rgyu 'bras dpe' don rtags dang rtan tshigs mthun par byas//rgyu'i dus na 'byung chen lnga byas pa la//'bras bu'i dus na 
183a2(1): khams gsum sems can byas//dpe' dus na kun dpe' nam mkha' byed//don kyis dus na kun skye myed byed//rtags kyi dus na kun byang chub sems//rtan tshigs dus na rang 'byung ye shes ston//rgyu 
183a3(1): 'bras dpe' don rtags dangs rtan tshigs mthun par bya//byang chub sems kyis kun byas gcig ma byas//kun byas byang chub sems kyi rang bzhin byas//gcig ma byas pa nyid las bya myi dgos//kun 
183a4(1): byed nga'i rang bzhin ma rtogs par//nga yis byas pa'i chos la nges brtags pas//rkam zhing chags pa'i ngang gis snang ba grub//myi rtag rgyu ma'i rang bzhin 'jig par 'gyur//cha myed dmu' long lta bu'i rang 
183a5(1): bzhin no//kun byed rgyal pos byas par myi cha bar//dbang po drug snang ba la//sdug bsngal mthong na dbang po 'gog par byed//des ni kun byed byas par myi chags//skye 'gag gnyis kyi 'bras bu bde 
183a6(1): la myed//nges pa gcig la bden pa gnyis byung pas//kun byed nga yis byas pa'i chos nyid la//don dam kun rdzob yin gnyis byung bas//bden pa gnyis kyi rgyu 'bras yod myi mnga'//myed par 
183a7(1): tshol ba dag pa'i ting 'dzin dang //myi 'byung cho 'phrul bsgoms mangs bas//blang dor myed pa'i 'bras bu thob pa myed//don cig ting 'dzin rnam gsum bsgom byed pas//bsnyen sgrub rnam gzhi rim
